- **gamelib-conventions**: GameLib's five load-bearing repo conventions in full, with the measurements and incident history behind each — todo triage frontmatter (the three keys CI enforces), fake-HOME isolation (the two-profile rule), the sidecar's exit contract (unref every handle; bound in-flight boot work), UAT item shape (inline `expected:`, `result:` opens with a status word), and the mandatory `npx prettier --check` in every task's `&lt;verify&gt;` block.
